📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Emerging microbes & infections  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-05  |  DOI: 10.1080/22221751.2026.2744490  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 2744490

物种广度是一个准备指标:2026 年本迪布焦病毒病 暴发 的教训。

Species Breadth Is a Preparedness Metric: Lessons from the 2026 Bundibugyo Virus Disease Outbreak.
作者： Ayan Mohamed Hassan, Abdullahi Abdi Yusuf, Adan Mohamed Hirsi, Ilyas Abdullahi Khalif, Ayaan Axmed Maxamed, Deka Ahmed Abdi
摘要中文翻译2026 年本迪布焦病毒病 暴发 在刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 和乌干达的快速、不受控制的增长不应仅仅被解释为延迟爆发的结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过检查 2026 年本迪布焦病毒病 暴发 来评估 埃博拉病毒 的准备指标,强调需要改进 监测 和应对策略,以防止此类 暴发 快速、不受控制的增长。
设计与对象
该研究分析了刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 和乌干达的 2026 年本迪布焦病毒病 暴发,重点关注流行病学模式及其对防备的影响。
核心方法
研究人员对 暴发 数据(包括流行病学和临床记录)进行了回顾性分析,以评估导致本迪布焦病毒病快速传播的因素。
主要结果
暴发 在遏制方面表现出了重大挑战,其快速增长归因于响应延迟和有限的 监测 能力。
解释与创新
作者将 暴发 解释为如何评估准备指标的关键示例,强调及时干预和强大的 监测 系统的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了加强 埃博拉病毒 准备策略的必要性,特别是在资源和基础设施有限的地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性和对报告数据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Species Breadth Is a Preparedness Metric: Lessons from the 2026 Bundibugyo Virus Disease Outbreak.

Authors: Ayan Mohamed Hassan, Abdullahi Abdi Yusuf, Adan Mohamed Hirsi, Ilyas Abdullahi Khalif, Ayaan Axmed Maxamed, Deka Ahmed Abdi
Original abstract (English)
The rapid, unchecked growth of the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) and Uganda should not be interpreted merely as a consequence of delayed outbr...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the preparedness metrics for Ebola viruses by examining the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak, highlighting the need for improved surveillance and response strategies to prevent rapid, unchecked growth of such outbreaks.
Design and population
The study analyzed the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) and Uganda, focusing on the epidemiological patterns and the implications for preparedness.
Core methods
The researchers conducted a retrospective analysis of outbreak data, including epidemiological and clinical records, to assess the factors contributing to the rapid spread of the Bundibugyo virus disease.
Main results
The outbreak demonstrated significant challenges in containment, with rapid growth attributed to delayed response and limited surveillance capabilities.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the outbreak as a critical example of how preparedness metrics can be evaluated, emphasizing the importance of timely intervention and robust surveillance systems.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the necessity of strengthening preparedness strategies for Ebola viruses, particularly in regions with limited resources and infrastructure.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective nature and reliance on reported data, which may introduce biases. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these constraints.

内源性抗 埃博拉病毒 GP1,2 抗体会干扰埃博拉病毒病患者血液中分泌糖蛋白的检测。

Endogenous anti-Ebola virus GP1,2 antibodies interfere with detection of secreted glycoprotein in the blood of EVD patients.
作者： Patrick T. Tshita, Aurélie Ploquin, John Misasi, Bobo N. Bazola, Samuel M. Shamamba, Hadar Marcus, Ange Y. Mubiala, Francis M. Ntumba, Saidou Milua, Courtney Tucker
摘要中文翻译摘要 埃博拉病(EBOD)是一种高度传染性疾病,具有严重的出血症状。它是由正埃博拉病毒引起的,其中包括导致 埃博拉病毒病 (EVD) 的 埃博拉病毒(扎伊尔正埃博拉病毒)(EBOV),这是对人类最致命的 EBOD。通过共转录聚合酶口吃和随后的蛋白水解加工,其 GP 基因产生多种蛋白质产物,包括分泌糖蛋白 (sGP) 和结构膜相关三聚体 GP 1,2。尽管已知 sGP 在血液中循环,但其在疾病病理学中的生理意义仍然是推测性的。我们使用酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA) 对刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 第 10 次和第 11 次 EVD 暴发 中的 36 例急性人类 EVD 病例使用单时间点生物库样本进行了 sGP 定量。我们在四名具有强 GP 基因转录(GP Ct 1,2 IgG)的患者中检测到并定量了 sGP。然而,在 GP 转录较低(Ct > 25)的患者中,无论其抗埃博拉病毒 GP 1,2 IgG 结果如何,都没有对 sGP 进行定量。而两名患者,MAM179 和 MAM271,具有明显强的 GP 转录(Ct 1,2 IgG 可能干扰了测定检测抗体的检测,此外,添加含有抗 EBOV GP 1,2 IgG 的血清可减少 sGP 与用作检测抗体的抗 EBOV GP 1,2 /sGP 之间的结合,这表明循环中 sGP 的水平与 GP 转录的强度密切相关。此外,在这种情况下,循环 sGP 会与抗 GP 1,2 抗体形成复合物,从而阻碍额外的抗体结合。重要性 深入了解 埃博拉病毒 分泌糖蛋白 (sGP) 在人体中的免疫功能机制对于更好地了解病毒 发病机制、免疫保护和免疫逃避策略至关重要。利用 埃博拉病毒 感染或感染后幸存患者的样本进行的研究为确定 sGP 在急性感染患者中的免疫学意义提供了无与伦比的机会。埃博拉病毒病 暴发,EBOV sGP 在调节针对病毒进入糖蛋白的抗体反应中的作用通过阐明 sGP 如何影响抗体介导的免疫,该研究增进了我们对所有正埃博拉病毒共有的保守免疫逃避机制的理解,这些研究是设计更有效的 疫苗、疗法和用于监测的生物标志物的宝贵资源。埃博拉病毒病。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究 EBOV sGP 在调节针对病毒进入糖蛋白的抗体反应中的作用,因为了解其机制对于更好地了解病毒 发病机制、免疫保护和免疫逃避策略至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究使用单时间点生物库样本分析了刚果民主共和国第 10 次和第 11 次 EVD 暴发 的 36 例急性人类 EVD 病例。
核心方法
使用酶联免疫吸附测定(ELISA)对 GP 基因转录强的患者进行 sGP 定量,同时通过添加含有抗 EBOV GP 1,2 IgG 的血清来测试 sGP 与检测抗体之间的结合。
主要结果
在 4 名具有强 GP 转录 (GP Ct 1,2 IgG) 的患者中检测到了 sGP 并进行了定量,但在 GP 转录较低 (Ct > 25) 的患者中未对其进行定量,无论其抗埃博拉病毒 GP 1,2 IgG 结果如何。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,循环中 sGP 的水平与 GP 转录的强度密切相关,并且循环中的 sGP 与抗 GP 1,2 抗体形成复合物,阻碍了额外的抗体结合以进行检测。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现是设计更有效的 疫苗、疗法和用于监测 埃博拉病毒病 的生物标志物的宝贵资源。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对单时间点生物库样本的依赖而受到限制,并且由于内源性抗 EBOV GP 1,2 抗体可能干扰测定,因此证据不充分。

Endogenous anti-Ebola virus GP1,2 antibodies interfere with detection of secreted glycoprotein in the blood of EVD patients.

Authors: Patrick T. Tshita, Aurélie Ploquin, John Misasi, Bobo N. Bazola, Samuel M. Shamamba, Hadar Marcus, Ange Y. Mubiala, Francis M. Ntumba, Saidou Milua, Courtney Tucker
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Ebola disease (EBOD) is a highly infectious disease with severe hemorrhagic symptoms. It is caused by orthoebolaviruses , which include the Ebola virus ( Orthoebolavirus zairense ) (EBOV) causing Ebola virus disease (EVD), the most lethal EBOD to humans. Through co-transcriptional polymerase stuttering and subsequent proteolytic processing, its GP gene produces multiple protein products, including secreted glycoprotein (sGP) and structural membrane-associated trimeric GP 1,2 . Although sGP is known to circulate in the blood, its physiological significance in disease pathology remains speculative. We quantified sGP using enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) in 36 acute human EVD cases from the 10th and 11th EVD outbreaks in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) using single-time-point biobanked samples. We detected and quantified sGP in four patients with strong GP gene transcription (GP Ct 1,2 IgG. However, sGP was not quantified in patients with lower GP transcription (Ct > 25), regardless of their anti-EBOV GP 1,2 IgG results. While two patients, MAM179 and MAM271, had notably strong GP transcription (Ct 1,2 IgG might have interfered with detection by the assay detection antibody, a human anti-EBOV GP 1,2 /sGP IgG. Furthermore, the addition of sera containing anti-EBOV GP 1,2 IgG reduced the binding between sGP and anti-EBOV GP 1,2 /sGP used as detection antibodies. This suggested that the level of sGP in circulation is strongly linked to the intensity of GP transcription. Additionally, circulating sGP in this context would form complexes with anti-GP 1,2 antibodies, hence hindering additional antibody binding for detection. IMPORTANCE Insight into the mechanism of immune function by the Ebola virus secreted glycoprotein (sGP) in humans is essential for better understanding viral pathogenesis, immune protection, and immune evasion strategies. Research utilizing samples from patients who are infected with or have survived infection with the Ebola virus offers an unparalleled opportunity to determine the immunological significance of sGP. This research evaluates, in acutely infected patients from Ebola virus disease outbreaks, the role of EBOV sGP in modulating antibody responses directed against viral entry glycoprotein. By elucidating how sGP influences antibody-mediated immunity, the study advances our understanding of a conserved immune-evasion mechanism shared across all orthoebolaviruses. These investigations are valuable resources for designing more efficacious vaccines, therapies, and biomarkers for monitoring Ebola virus diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of EBOV sGP in modulating antibody responses directed against viral entry glycoprotein, as understanding its mechanism is essential for better understanding viral pathogenesis, immune protection, and immune evasion strategies.
Design and population
The study analyzed 36 acute human EVD cases from the 10th and 11th EVD outbreaks in the Democratic Republic of the Congo using single-time-point biobanked samples.
Core methods
sGP was quantified using enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) in patients with strong GP gene transcription, while the binding between sGP and detection antibodies was tested by adding sera containing anti-EBOV GP 1,2 IgG.
Main results
sGP was detected and quantified in four patients with strong GP transcription (GP Ct 1,2 IgG), but it was not quantified in patients with lower GP transcription (Ct > 25), regardless of their anti-EBOV GP 1,2 IgG results.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the level of sGP in circulation is strongly linked to the intensity of GP transcription, and that circulating sGP forms complexes with anti-GP 1,2 antibodies, hindering additional antibody binding for detection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings are valuable resources for designing more efficacious vaccines, therapies, and biomarkers for monitoring Ebola virus diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on single-time-point biobanked samples, and the evidence is moderate due to the potential for assay interference by endogenous anti-EBOV GP 1,2 antibodies.

VSV-EBOV 疫苗接种 在人体中诱导针对本迪布焦病毒的交叉反应 T 细胞反应

VSV-EBOV vaccination induces cross-reactive T-cell responses against Bundibugyo virus in humans
作者： Leonie Mayer, Rolanda Londt, Eva Gruener, My Linh Ly, Marylyn M. Addo, Christof Geldmacher
摘要中文翻译摘要 本迪布焦埃博拉病毒 (BDBV) 正在刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 引发持续的 暴发,且没有获得许可的 疫苗。最近的报告显示,接种相关 埃博拉病毒 (EBOV) 疫苗的人类中存在 BDBV 交叉反应性抗体反应。在这里,我们使用 IFN-γ ELISpot 测定来评估接种许可的 VSV-EBOV 疫苗 疫苗的临床试验参与者对 EBOV、BDBV 和 SUDV 糖蛋白的 T 细胞反应。我们可以证明,在大多数个体中可以检测到 BDBV 交叉反应性 T 细胞反应,并且糖蛋白内的保守区域是目标。在缺乏 BDBV 特异性 疫苗 的情况下,这些数据支持当前 暴发 期间 VSV-EBOV 疫苗 的功效试验。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于刚果民主共和国正在进行的 暴发 且缺乏获得许可的 疫苗,该研究旨在调查 VSV-EBOV 疫苗 是否可以诱导针对本迪布焦病毒 (BDBV) 的交叉反应性 T 细胞反应。
设计与对象
该研究是使用已接种许可的 VSV-EBOV 疫苗 疫苗的临床试验参与者进行的。该研究的重点是评估 T 细胞针对 BDBV 和相关病毒(如 EBOV 和 SUDV)的反应。
核心方法
研究人员采用 IFN-γ ELISpot 测定来评估接种疫苗的个体中 T 细胞对 EBOV、BDBV 和 SUDV 糖蛋白的反应。
主要结果
结果表明,在大多数个体中可检测到 BDBV 交叉反应性 T 细胞反应,其中糖蛋白内的保守区域是主要目标。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,VSV-EBOV 疫苗 可能对 BDBV 提供一些保护作用,因为它诱导交叉反应性 T 细胞反应,这是 疫苗 相关 埃博拉病毒 开发背景下的新颖见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为 VSV-EBOV 疫苗 在控制 BDBV 暴发 中的潜在用途提供了重要信息,支持在当前 暴发 期间进行进一步疗效试验的需要。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于交叉反应性 T 细胞反应,而不是直接的病毒攻击或临床结果,而且由于缺乏长期随访数据,证据并不充分。

VSV-EBOV vaccination induces cross-reactive T-cell responses against Bundibugyo virus in humans

Authors: Leonie Mayer, Rolanda Londt, Eva Gruener, My Linh Ly, Marylyn M. Addo, Christof Geldmacher
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Bundibugyo ebolavirus (BDBV) is causing an ongoing outbreak in the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC) with no licensed vaccine available. Recent reports have shown the presence of BDBV-cross-reactive antibody responses in humans vaccinated against the related Ebola virus (EBOV). Here, we used an IFN-γ ELISpot assay to assess T cell responses against the EBOV, BDBV and SUDV glycoproteins in clinical trial participants vaccinated with the licensed VSV-EBOV vaccine. We could show that BDBV-cross-reactive T cell responses are detectable in the majority of individuals and that conserved regions within the glycoprotein are targeted. In the absence of a BDBV-specific vaccine, these data support efficacy trials of the VSV-EBOV vaccine during the current outbreak.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether the VSV-EBOV vaccine could induce cross-reactive T-cell responses against Bundibugyo virus (BDBV), given the ongoing outbreak in the Democratic Republic of Congo and the lack of a licensed vaccine.
Design and population
The study was conducted using clinical trial participants who had been vaccinated with the licensed VSV-EBOV vaccine. The research focused on assessing T-cell responses against BDBV and related viruses such as EBOV and SUDV.
Core methods
The researchers employed an IFN-γ ELISpot assay to evaluate T-cell responses against the glycoproteins of EBOV, BDBV, and SUDV in vaccinated individuals.
Main results
The results demonstrated that BDBV-cross-reactive T-cell responses were detectable in the majority of individuals, with conserved regions within the glycoprotein being the primary targets.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the VSV-EBOV vaccine may offer some protective benefit against BDBV, as it induces cross-reactive T-cell responses, which is a novel insight in the context of vaccine development for related Ebola viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide important information for the potential use of the VSV-EBOV vaccine in controlling BDBV outbreaks, supporting the need for further efficacy trials during current outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on cross-reactive T-cell responses rather than direct viral challenge or clinical outcomes, and the evidence is moderate due to the absence of long-term follow-up data.

Journal: Bulletin of the World Health Organization  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-01  |  DOI: 10.2471/blt.26.296654  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 104, 10, 658-658A

本迪布焦病毒病和未完成的对策议程。

Bundibugyo virus disease and the unfinished countermeasure agenda.

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究强调需要解决 埃博拉病毒 对策方面的差距,特别强调本迪布焦病毒病尚未完成的议程。作者指出,虽然在针对其他 埃博拉病毒 物种(例如扎伊尔埃博拉病毒)开发 疫苗 方面取得了进展,但仍然缺乏针对本迪布焦病毒的有效干预措施。
设计与对象
该研究是一篇社论,回顾了 埃博拉病毒 对策的研究和开发现状,特别关注本迪布焦病毒。它讨论了 埃博拉病毒病 的更广泛背景以及为该特定物种创建靶向疗法和 疫苗 的挑战。
核心方法
社论没有描述具体方法或实验程序。相反,它综合了现有文献,并评估了包括本迪布焦病毒在内的各种 埃博拉病毒 物种的对策开发进展。
主要结果
该社论报道称,尽管在为其他 埃博拉病毒 物种开发 疫苗 方面取得了一些进展,但目前还没有批准用于 Bundibugyo 病毒的 疫苗 或特定 治疗。它还指出,该疾病与乌干达的 暴发 有关,缺乏有效的干预措施仍然是一个关键问题。
解释与创新
作者解释说,缺乏针对本迪布焦病毒病的有效对策凸显了进一步研究和开发的必要性。他们强调这篇社论的新颖之处在于强调了这种特定病毒未完成的议程。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明优先研究本迪布焦病毒对策的重要性,这对于改进针对这种新兴病原体的 监测、诊断 和 治疗 策略至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于编辑审查,不提供原始数据。这些证据基于现有文献,这为有关本迪布焦病毒对策开发现状的结论提供了中等程度的可信度。

View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study highlights the need for addressing the gaps in countermeasures for Ebola viruses, particularly emphasizing the unfinished agenda for Bundibugyo virus disease. The authors note that while progress has been made in developing vaccines for other Ebola virus species, such as Zaire ebolavirus, there remains a lack of effective interventions for Bundibugyo virus.
Design and population
The study is an editorial that reviews the current state of research and development on countermeasures for Ebola viruses, with a specific focus on Bundibugyo virus. It discusses the broader context of Ebola virus disease and the challenges in creating targeted therapies and vaccines for this particular species.
Core methods
The editorial does not describe specific methods or experimental procedures. Instead, it synthesizes existing literature and evaluates the progress made in countermeasure development for various Ebola virus species, including Bundibugyo virus.
Main results
The editorial reports that there is currently no approved vaccine or specific treatment for Bundibugyo virus, despite some progress in developing vaccines for other Ebola virus species. It also notes that the disease has been associated with outbreaks in Uganda and that the lack of effective interventions remains a critical issue.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the lack of effective countermeasures for Bundibugyo virus disease underscores the need for further research and development. They emphasize the novelty of this editorial in highlighting the unfinished agenda for this specific virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of prioritizing research into countermeasures for Bundibugyo virus, which is critical for improving surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for this emerging pathogen.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an editorial review and does not present original data. The evidence is based on existing literature, which provides a moderate level of confidence in the conclusions drawn about the current state of countermeasure development for Bundibugyo virus.

刚果民主共和国本迪布焦病毒病的快速地理扩张、增长减速和六个月情景预测:情况报告分析 1-61

Rapid Geographic Expansion, Decelerating Growth, and Six-Month Scenario Projections of Bundibugyo Virus Disease in the Democratic Republic of the Congo: Analysis of Situation Reports 1–61
作者： David Lupande Mwenebitu, Demelash Areda, Pascal Adroba, Franklin Mwamba, Philippe Boribo Kikunda, Olivier Mukuku, Danstan Bagenda, Misaki Wayengera, Patrick D. M. Katoto, Gaylord Ngaboyeka
摘要中文翻译2026年5月,刚果民主共和国宣布由本迪布焦埃博拉病毒引起的本迪布焦病毒病为暴发。我们分析了 2026 年 5 月 14 日至 7 月 14 日发布的汇总官方情况报告 (SitReps),以描述报告的流行病轨迹、地理扩张、死亡率、特定区间增长和有条件的六个月预测。确诊病例从8例增加到2073例,确诊死亡人数达到796例;5个省的45个卫生区受到影响。尽管累积病例和地理足迹持续增加,但在观察期内,平均特定区间增长率普遍下降。根据均方根误差(RMSE)、修正的赤池信息准则(AICc)和贝叶斯信息准则(BIC),Gompertz和Richards曲线比Logistic和指数曲线更好地描述了观测到的累积轨迹;此比较仅反映样本内拟合。有条件的六个月情景在不同假设之间存在很大差异(截至 2027 年 1 月 14 日累计病例数约为 2435 至 10,839 例),且具有偶然性且高度不确定;Gompertz 拟合显示对四次时间检查中随后报告的总数的预测不足。各省报告的病死率(CFR)各不相同,但很容易受到结果延迟、病例确定不完整、分母较小和回顾性重新分类的影响。结果支持持续的 监测 和应对计划,但没有确定疫情放缓的原因或确定 传播 已得到控制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解刚果民主共和国本迪布焦病毒病的地理扩张和增长动态,并预测其在未来六个月内的潜在传播。
设计与对象
该研究分析了2026年5月14日至7月14日发布的汇总官方情况报告(SitReps),涵盖受暴发影响的五个省份的45个卫生区。
核心方法
研究人员使用 SitReps 来描述流行病轨迹、地理扩张、死亡率、特定时间间隔的增长和有条件的六个月预测,并采用统计模型来拟合数据。
主要结果
确诊病例从 8 例增加到 2073 例,确诊死亡人数达到 796 例。覆盖范围扩大到五个省份的 45 个卫生区。有条件的六个月情景差异很大,预计到 2027 年 1 月 14 日累计病例数将在 2435 例至 10,839 例之间。
解释与创新
研究发现,基于样本内拟合指标,Gompertz 和 Richards 曲线比 Logistic 曲线和指数曲线更好地描述了累积轨迹。然而,这些预测具有高度不确定性并且取决于假设。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持需要继续制定 监测 和针对本迪布焦病毒病的应对计划,但它们没有提供关于流行病减缓的原因或 传播 控制措施有效性的明确见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到对汇总 SitReps 的依赖的限制,这可能会导致结果延迟、病例确定不完整、分母较小和回顾性重新分类。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Rapid Geographic Expansion, Decelerating Growth, and Six-Month Scenario Projections of Bundibugyo Virus Disease in the Democratic Republic of the Congo: Analysis of Situation Reports 1–61

Authors: David Lupande Mwenebitu, Demelash Areda, Pascal Adroba, Franklin Mwamba, Philippe Boribo Kikunda, Olivier Mukuku, Danstan Bagenda, Misaki Wayengera, Patrick D. M. Katoto, Gaylord Ngaboyeka
Original abstract (English)
In May 2026, the Democratic Republic of the Congo declared an outbreak of Bundibugyo virus disease caused by Bundibugyo ebolavirus. We analysed aggregated official Situation Reports (SitReps) issued from 14 May through 14 July 2026 to describe the reported epidemic trajectory, geographic expansion, mortality, interval-specific growth, and conditional six-month projections. Confirmed cases increased from 8 to 2073 and confirmed deaths reached 796; 45 health zones in five provinces were affected. Average interval-specific growth rates generally declined over the observation period, although cumulative cases and the geographic footprint continued to increase. Gompertz and Richards curves described the observed cumulative trajectory better than logistic and exponential curves according to root mean square error (RMSE), the corrected Akaike information criterion (AICc), and the Bayesian information criterion (BIC); this comparison reflects in-sample fit only. Conditional six-month scenarios varied substantially across assumptions (approximately 2435 to 10,839 cumulative cases by 14 January 2027) and are contingent and highly uncertain; Gompertz fits showed underprediction of the subsequently reported total in four temporal checks. Reported case fatality ratios (CFRs) varied across provinces but are vulnerable to outcome delay, incomplete case ascertainment, small denominators, and retrospective reclassification. The results support continued surveillance and response planning but do not identify the causes of epidemic slowing or establish that transmission was controlled.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the geographic expansion and growth dynamics of Bundibugyo virus disease in the Democratic Republic of the Congo, as well as to project its potential spread over the next six months.
Design and population
The study analyzed aggregated official Situation Reports (SitReps) issued from 14 May through 14 July 2026, covering 45 health zones in five provinces affected by the outbreak.
Core methods
The researchers used SitReps to describe the epidemic trajectory, geographic expansion, mortality, interval-specific growth, and conditional six-month projections, employing statistical models to fit the data.
Main results
Confirmed cases increased from 8 to 2073, with confirmed deaths reaching 796. The geographic footprint expanded to 45 health zones across five provinces. Conditional six-month scenarios varied substantially, with cumulative cases projected to range from 2435 to 10,839 by 14 January 2027.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that Gompertz and Richards curves better described the cumulative trajectory than logistic and exponential curves, based on in-sample fit metrics. However, the projections were highly uncertain and contingent on assumptions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the need for continued surveillance and response planning for Bundibugyo virus disease, but they do not provide definitive insights into the causes of the epidemic slowing or the effectiveness of transmission control measures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the reliance on aggregated SitReps, which may have outcome delays, incomplete case ascertainment, small denominators, and retrospective reclassification. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

NEXTEBOVAX: ONTCIJFERING VAN ORTHOEBOLAVIRUS-IMMUNITEIT 机制研究:同源与异源对正埃博拉病毒的保护作用

NEXTEBOVAX: ONTCIJFERING VAN ORTHOEBOLAVIRUS-IMMUNITEIT Mechanismen van homologe en heterologe bescherming tegen orthoebolavirussen in preklinische modellen
作者： Lara Kelchtermans
摘要中文翻译丝状病毒科由抗原性不同的人类致病病毒组成,例如 埃博拉病毒、马尔堡病毒和苏丹病毒。丝状病毒已经进化出复杂的方法来逃避 免疫应答,从而导致高 死亡率 率,并且 暴发 在过去几十年中不断出现。只有两种 疫苗 已获得欧洲药品管理局 (EMA) 的 埃博拉病毒 市场授权,但其他高致病性丝状病毒尚未获得市场授权。本博士论文的目的是研究转基因黄热病 17D 载体 疫苗 候选药物在小动物模型和小动物模型血清中的保护功效,该候选药物针对不同丝状病毒的糖蛋白。为此,我们将利用假型丝状病毒,例如携带这些丝状病毒糖蛋白的重组水泡性口炎病毒,可在生物安全2级设施中使用,以及评估实验性疫苗候选物引起的免疫应答的广泛测定,以最终确定这些疫苗候选物的保护功效的质量。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究转基因黄热病 17D 载体 疫苗 候选物对不同丝状病毒的保护功效,因为这些病毒引起的高 死亡率 率和反复出现的 暴发。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注小动物模型,并利用这些模型衍生的血清来评估针对各种丝状病毒糖蛋白的 疫苗 候选药物,包括 埃博拉病毒 及其相关物种。
核心方法
该研究采用了假型丝状病毒,例如携带这些丝状病毒糖蛋白的重组水泡性口炎病毒,并进行了广泛的测定来评估实验性 疫苗 候选物引发的 免疫应答。
主要结果
使用假型丝状病毒和小动物模型血清评估了 疫苗 候选药物的保护功效,目的是确定其针对目标丝状病毒的有效性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,疫苗 候选药物可以针对目标丝状病毒提供保护,有助于了解针对这些病原体的免疫机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为针对丝状病毒开发更有效的 疫苗 提供信息,特别是在临床前环境中,并支持未来对更广泛的免疫保护策略的研究。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于临床前模型,并未提供人类功效的直接证据,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。证据强度中等。

NEXTEBOVAX: ONTCIJFERING VAN ORTHOEBOLAVIRUS-IMMUNITEIT Mechanismen van homologe en heterologe bescherming tegen orthoebolavirussen in preklinische modellen

Authors: Lara Kelchtermans
Original abstract (English)
The family Filoviridae consists of antigenically distinct human pathogenic viruses such as, among others, Ebola virus, Marburg virus and Sudan virus. Filoviruses have evolved sophisticated ways of evading the immune response thereby causing high mortality rates, and outbreaks are recurring over the past decades. Only two vaccines have been granted market authorization by the European Medicines Agency (EMA) for Ebola virus, yet none for the other highly pathogenic filoviruses. The aim of this PhD thesis is to study the protective efficacy of transgenic yellow fever 17D vectored vaccine candidates targeting the glycoprotein of the distinct filoviruses in small animal models, and with sera derived from small animal models. To that end, we will make use of pseudotyped filoviruses, such as recombinant vesicular stomatitis viruses carrying the glycoprotein of these filoviruses, which can be used within biosafety level 2 facilities, as well as a wide range of assays to evaluate the immune response elicited by the experimental vaccine candidates to ultimately determine the quality of the protective efficacy of these vaccine candidates.​
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the protective efficacy of transgenic yellow fever 17D vectored vaccine candidates against distinct filoviruses, given the high mortality rates and recurring outbreaks caused by these viruses.
Design and population
The research focused on small animal models and utilized sera derived from these models to evaluate vaccine candidates targeting the glycoprotein of various filoviruses, including Ebola virus and its related species.
Core methods
The study employed pseudotyped filoviruses, such as recombinant vesicular stomatitis viruses carrying the glycoprotein of these filoviruses, and conducted a wide range of assays to assess the immune response elicited by the experimental vaccine candidates.
Main results
The protective efficacy of the vaccine candidates was evaluated using pseudotyped filoviruses and sera from small animal models, with the goal of determining their effectiveness against the targeted filoviruses.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the vaccine candidates may offer protection against the targeted filoviruses, contributing to the understanding of immune mechanisms against these pathogens.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results may inform the development of more effective vaccines for filoviruses, particularly in preclinical settings, and support future research into broader immune protection strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to preclinical models and does not provide direct evidence on human efficacy, which may affect the generalisability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

模型引导的分散诊断放置,用于 2026 年本迪布焦埃博拉疫情的空间控制

Model-guided placement of decentralized diagnostics for spatial control of the 2026 Bundibugyo Ebola epidemic
作者： Daniel J. Klein, Kyra H. Grantz, Caroline O. Buckee, Dylan B. George, Scott F. Dowell, Klein, D. J., Grantz, K. H., Buckee, C. O., George, D. B., Dowell, S. F.
摘要中文翻译2026 年之前,本迪布焦病毒病只有两个公认的 暴发。到 2026 年 8 月,刚果民主共和国东部的延迟识别和多天的样本运输导致了大规模、空间分散的流行病。我们假设,在边境卫生区以模型为指导放置分散诊断可以通过充当空间围栏来帮助遏制空间传播:在输入谱系建立之前,延迟识别将转化为及时的聚合酶链反应(PCR)确认、隔离和接触者追踪。我们将贝叶斯空间分支过程与截至 2026 年 8 月 2 日的公共状况报告相匹配,其中包括 Flowminder 运动和潜在负担锚。谱系有过度分散的后代、PCR 获取和周转、临床识别、隔离、追踪、安全埋葬和当地行为改变。由于观察到的历史并不能确定未来的控制,我们假设国家响应斜坡产生西非规模的现状负担,并通过 5-100 个区域级 PCR 实验室重播了 2,000 棵后树,部署延迟为 0-180 天,并进行了事后快速诊断测试 (RDT) 覆盖。后面再现的病例、死亡、潜在负担和空间目标。速度占主导地位:如果立即部署,35 个 PCR 实验室可避免未来死亡的中位数为 49%,14 天后可避免 38%,60 天后可避免 11%,90% 预测区间分别为 30-65%、23-53% 和 5-20%。安置也很重要:延迟 30 天后,在 20-100 个实验室中,基于模型的安置可以避免 18-33% 的未来死亡,而随机安置则为 1-8%。尸检 RDT 部分填补了活体病例检测留下的空白,通过 20 个实验室、14 天的延迟和 75% 的覆盖率,将未来的死亡人数从 26% 提高到 41%。当分散的诊断先于可能的输入并与人员配备、隔离、追踪和安全埋葬能力相结合时,可以减缓空间传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
我们将贝叶斯空间分支过程与截至 2026 年 8 月 2 日的公共状况报告相匹配,其中包括 Flowminder 运动和潜在负担锚。
设计与对象
谱系有过度分散的后代、PCR 获取和周转、临床识别、隔离、追踪、安全埋葬和当地行为改变。
核心方法
我们假设,在边境卫生区以模型为指导放置分散诊断可以通过充当空间围栏来帮助遏制空间传播:在输入谱系建立之前,延迟识别将转化为及时的聚合酶链反应(PCR)确认、隔离和接触者追踪。
主要结果
由于观察到的历史并不能确定未来的控制,我们假设国家响应斜坡产生西非规模的现状负担,并通过 5-100 个区域级 PCR 实验室重播了 2,000 棵后树,部署延迟为 0-180 天,并进行了事后快速诊断测试 (RDT) 覆盖。
解释与创新
安置也很重要:延迟 30 天后,在 20-100 个实验室中,基于模型的安置可以避免 18-33% 的未来死亡,而随机安置则为 1-8%。
科研与公卫意义
尸检 RDT 部分填补了活体病例检测留下的空白,通过 20 个实验室、14 天的延迟和 75% 的覆盖率,将未来的死亡人数从 26% 提高到 41%。
局限与证据强度
当分散的诊断先于可能的输入并与人员配备、隔离、追踪和安全埋葬能力相结合时,可以减缓空间传播。

Model-guided placement of decentralized diagnostics for spatial control of the 2026 Bundibugyo Ebola epidemic

Authors: Daniel J. Klein, Kyra H. Grantz, Caroline O. Buckee, Dylan B. George, Scott F. Dowell, Klein, D. J., Grantz, K. H., Buckee, C. O., George, D. B., Dowell, S. F.
Original abstract (English)
Before 2026, Bundibugyo virus disease had only two recognized outbreaks. By August 2026, delayed recognition and multi-day sample transport in eastern Democratic Republic of the Congo had contributed to a large, spatially dispersed epidemic. We hypothesized that model-guided placement of decentralized diagnostics in frontier health zones could help contain spatial spread by acting as a spatial fence: delayed recognition would be converted into timely polymerase chain reaction (PCR) confirmation, isolation, and contact tracing before imported lineages became established. We fit a Bayesian spatial branching process to public situation reports through 2 August 2026, with Flowminder movement and a latent-burden anchor. Lineages had overdispersed offspring, PCR access and turnaround, clinical recognition, isolation, tracing, safe burial, and local behaviour change. Because the observed history did not identify future control, we assumed a national response ramp yielding West-Africa-scale status-quo burden and replayed 2,000 posterior trees with 5-100 zone-level PCR laboratories, deployment delays of 0-180 days, and postmortem rapid diagnostic test (RDT) coverage. The posterior reproduced case, death, latent-burden, and spatial targets. Speed dominated: 35 PCR laboratories averted a median 49% of future deaths if deployed immediately, 38% after 14 days, and 11% after 60 days, with 90% prediction intervals of 30-65%, 23-53%, and 5-20%, respectively. Placement also mattered: with a 30-day delay, model-informed placement averted 18-33% of future deaths with 20-100 laboratories, versus 1-8% under random placement. Postmortem RDTs partly filled gaps left by live-case detection, increasing future deaths averted from 26% to 41% with 20 laboratories, a 14-day delay, and 75% coverage. Decentralized diagnostics can slow spatial spread when they are placed ahead of likely importations and paired with staffing, isolation, tracing, and safe-burial capacity.

We fit a Bayesian spatial branching process to public situation reports through 2 August 2026, with Flowminder movement and a latent-burden anchor.
Design and population

Core methods
We hypothesized that model-guided placement of decentralized diagnostics in frontier health zones could help contain spatial spread by acting as a spatial fence: delayed recognition would be converted into timely polymerase chain reaction (PCR) confirmation, isolation, and contact tracing before imported lineages became established.
Main results
Because the observed history did not identify future control, we assumed a national response ramp yielding West-Africa-scale status-quo burden and replayed 2,000 posterior trees with 5-100 zone-level PCR laboratories, deployment delays of 0-180 days, and postmortem rapid diagnostic test (RDT) coverage.
Interpretation and novelty
Placement also mattered: with a 30-day delay, model-informed placement averted 18-33% of future deaths with 20-100 laboratories, versus 1-8% under random placement.
Scientific and public-health significance
Postmortem RDTs partly filled gaps left by live-case detection, increasing future deaths averted from 26% to 41% with 20 laboratories, a 14-day delay, and 75% coverage.

刚果民主共和国未受影响城市的医护人员对埃博拉病毒的恐惧和埃博拉病毒期间工作的意愿 暴发

Fear of Ebola and willingness to work during an Ebola outbreak among healthcare workers in a non-affected city of the Democratic Republic of the Congo
作者： Peniel Masele-Kyaite, Marc Ilunga-Kasambayi, Marilyn-Maryse Ngoy, Philippe Mulenga-Cilundika
摘要中文翻译摘要简介:刚果民主共和国正在发生的埃博拉病毒 暴发 凸显了评估医护人员的应对准备情况的迫切需要,因为他们构成了 埃博拉病毒病 暴发 期间特别暴露的人群。本研究旨在评估刚果民主共和国(刚果民主共和国)卢本巴希对埃博拉病毒的恐惧程度,并分析其与在可能出现 暴发 疫情时的工作意愿之间的关系。方法 2026 年 5 月 24 日至 6 月 22 日,对在卢本巴希市执业的医护人员进行了一项包含分析成分的描述性横断面观察研究,卢本巴希市并未直接受到刚果民主共和国正在发生的埃博拉病毒 暴发 的影响。采用方便抽样法,纳入了 135 名参与者。使用两个 10 项量表评估对埃博拉的恐惧和工作意愿,具有令人满意的内部一致性(Cronbach’s α:分别为 0.75 和 0.86)。使用双变量分析和多变量逻辑回归评估关联性。结果 参与者的中位年龄为 30 岁,中位专业经验为 4 年;63.7% 为男性,67.4% 为医生。恐惧尤其与对亲属安全(40.3/50)和将埃博拉病毒传播给家庭成员的风险(39.0/50)的担忧相关。在双变量分析中,对埃博拉病毒的恐惧与工作意愿呈负相关(Spearman’s rho = − 0.34;p
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
刚果民主共和国正在发生的埃博拉病毒 暴发 突显了评估医护人员的应对准备情况的迫切需要,因为他们构成了 埃博拉病毒病 暴发 期间特别暴露的人群。
设计与对象
2026 年 5 月 24 日至 6 月 22 日,对在刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 卢本巴希市执业的医护人员进行了一项包含分析成分的描述性横断面观察研究。
核心方法
采用方便抽样法,纳入了 135 名参与者。使用两个 10 项量表评估对埃博拉的恐惧和工作意愿,具有令人满意的内部一致性(Cronbach’s α:分别为 0.75 和 0.86)。使用双变量分析和多变量逻辑回归评估关联性。
主要结果
参与者的中位年龄为 30 岁,中位专业经验为 4 年;63.7% 为男性,67.4% 为医生。恐惧尤其与对亲属安全(40.3/50)和将埃博拉病毒传播给家庭成员的风险(39.0/50)的担忧相关。在双变量分析中,对埃博拉病毒的恐惧与工作意愿呈负相关(Spearman’s rho = − 0.34;p. 1)。
解释与创新
研究发现,对埃博拉病毒的恐惧与工作意愿呈负相关,这表明医护人员的恐惧可能会影响他们在埃博拉疫情期间应对的准备程度。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为解决医护人员的恐惧并提高他们在埃博拉 暴发 期间的工作准备度的策略提供参考,这对于有效的应对和控制措施至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究使用便利抽样可能会引入选择偏差,并且研究结果可能无法推广到其他人群或环境。证据强度中等。

Fear of Ebola and willingness to work during an Ebola outbreak among healthcare workers in a non-affected city of the Democratic Republic of the Congo

Authors: Peniel Masele-Kyaite, Marc Ilunga-Kasambayi, Marilyn-Maryse Ngoy, Philippe Mulenga-Cilundika
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction : The ongoing Ebola outbreak in the DRC highlights the urgent need to assess healthcare workers’ readiness to respond, as they constitute a particularly exposed population during Ebola virus disease outbreaks. This study aimed to assess the level of fear of Ebola and to analyse its association with willingness to work in the event of a potential outbreak in Lubumbashi, Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC). Methods A descriptive cross-sectional observational study with an analytical component was conducted from 24 May to 22 June 2026 among healthcare workers practising in the city of Lubumbashi, a city not directly affected by the ongoing Ebola outbreak in the DRC. Convenience sampling was used to include 135 participants. Fear of Ebola and willingness to work were assessed using two 10-item scales, with satisfactory internal consistency (Cronbach’s α: 0.75 and 0.86, respectively). Associations were assessed using bivariate analyses and multivariable logistic regression. Results The median age of participants was 30 years and the median professional experience was 4 years; 63.7% were men and 67.4% were physicians. Fear was particularly related to concerns about the safety of relatives (40.3/50) and the risk of transmitting Ebola to family members (39.0/50). In bivariate analysis, fear of Ebola was negatively associated with willingness to work (Spearman’s rho = − 0.34 ; p
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The ongoing Ebola outbreak in the DRC highlights the urgent need to assess healthcare workers’ readiness to respond, as they constitute a particularly exposed population during Ebola virus disease outbreaks.
Design and population
A descriptive cross-sectional observational study with an analytical component was conducted from 24 May to 22 June 2026 among healthcare workers practising in the city of Lubumbashi, Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC).
Core methods
Convenience sampling was used to include 135 participants. Fear of Ebola and willingness to work were assessed using two 10-item scales, with satisfactory internal consistency (Cronbach’s α: 0.75 and 0.86, respectively). Associations were assessed using bivariate analyses and multivariable logistic regression.
Main results
The median age of participants was 30 years and the median professional experience was 4 years; 63.7% were men and 67.4% were physicians. Fear was particularly related to concerns about the safety of relatives (40.3/50) and the risk of transmitting Ebola to family members (39.0/50). In bivariate analysis, fear of Ebola was negatively associated with willingness to work (Spearman’s rho = − 0.34 ; p.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that fear of Ebola was negatively associated with willingness to work, indicating that healthcare workers’ fear may impact their readiness to respond during an Ebola outbreak.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform strategies to address healthcare workers’ fears and improve their readiness to work during an Ebola outbreak, which is crucial for effective response and control measures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s use of convenience sampling may introduce selection bias, and the findings may not be generalizable to other populations or settings. The evidence strength is moderate.

非洲果蝠对表达埃博拉或尼帕病毒抗原的重组 疫苗 免疫攻击的血清学和转录组反应

Serological and transcriptomic response of African fruit bats to immune challenge with recombinant vaccines expressing Ebola or Nipah virus antigens
作者： Elinor Jax, Romain Garnier, Georgia Tsagkogeorga, Hubert Buczkowski, Louise Gibson, Pius Benia, Richard Suu-Ire, Vera M. Warmuth, Sylvester Languon, Osbourne Quaye
摘要中文翻译摘要 蝙蝠是多种人畜共患病病毒的宿主,这些病毒可导致人类高病死率。由于在蝙蝠体内检测病毒很困难,因此经常使用抗体来检测 监测,但在缺乏体内免疫学研究的情况下,其意义仍不清楚。为了解决这一知识差距,我们测量了注射编码尼帕病毒 G 糖蛋白或 埃博拉病毒 GP 糖蛋白的 疫苗 后蝙蝠体内抗体的产生和基因表达。我们选择了分布范围重叠的两种非洲蝙蝠物种:Eidolon helvum(确认为亨尼帕病毒 储存宿主)和 Epomophorus gambianus(假定为丝状病毒 储存宿主)。使用基于 Luminex 的多重微球免疫测定和假型病毒中和测定,我们观察到大多数接受 Nipah 疫苗 的蝙蝠体内针对 Nipah 糖蛋白的抗体有所增加。相比之下,我们只能在一种蝙蝠(Ep.)中检测到抗埃博拉糖蛋白抗体。gambianus,针对埃博拉病毒 疫苗。Ei 中的转录组分析。helvum 揭示,参与 ISGylation 和 T 细胞分化途径的基因在对埃博拉病毒的反应中存在差异表达,但对尼帕病毒 疫苗 则没有差异表达。这些结果证明了对抗原暴露反应的物种特异性差异,提供了对蝙蝠适应性免疫系统的深入了解,并为野生蝙蝠的血清学数据提供了校准。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决暴露于编码埃博拉病毒或尼帕病毒抗原的 疫苗 后蝙蝠中抗体产生和基因表达的知识差距,因为在蝙蝠中检测病毒很困难,并且在没有体内免疫学研究的情况下抗体的意义仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究涉及测量两种非洲蝙蝠——Eidolon helvum(确认为亨尼帕病毒的 储存宿主)和 Epomophorus gambianus(假定为丝状病毒的 储存宿主)在注射表达尼帕病毒 G 糖蛋白或 埃博拉病毒 GP 糖蛋白的 疫苗 后的抗体产生和基因表达。
核心方法
使用基于 Luminex 的多重微球免疫测定和假型病毒中和测定评估抗体产生,同时进行转录组分析以评估响应 疫苗 的基因表达变化。
主要结果
大多数接受 Nipah 疫苗 的蝙蝠表现出针对 Nipah 糖蛋白的抗体增加,而只有一种蝙蝠物种(Epomophorus gambianus)表现出可检测到的针对埃博拉 疫苗 的抗埃博拉糖蛋白抗体。Eidolon helvum 中的转录组分析揭示了埃博拉 疫苗 后参与 ISGylation 和 T 细胞分化途径的基因的差异表达,但尼帕 疫苗 则不然。
解释与创新
结果证明了 免疫应答 对抗原暴露的物种特异性差异,提供了对蝙蝠适应性免疫系统的深入了解,并为野生蝙蝠种群的血清学数据提供了校准。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为蝙蝠 免疫应答 丝状病毒抗原提供了宝贵的见解,可以为野生蝙蝠血清学数据的解释提供信息,并有助于了解 埃博拉病毒 的 传播 动态。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,并且结果基于少数蝙蝠物种和个体,这可能会影响普遍性。由于缺乏详细的体内免疫学研究,证据强度中等。

Serological and transcriptomic response of African fruit bats to immune challenge with recombinant vaccines expressing Ebola or Nipah virus antigens

Authors: Elinor Jax, Romain Garnier, Georgia Tsagkogeorga, Hubert Buczkowski, Louise Gibson, Pius Benia, Richard Suu-Ire, Vera M. Warmuth, Sylvester Languon, Osbourne Quaye
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Bats are hosts to diverse zoonotic viruses which can cause high case fatality rates in humans. Since detecting viruses in bats is difficult, antibodies are often used for surveillance, though their significance remains unclear in the absence of in vivo immunological studies. To address this knowledge gap, we measured antibody production and gene expression in bats following injection with vaccines encoding either a Nipah virus G glycoprotein or an Ebola virus GP glycoprotein. We selected two African bat species with overlapping ranges, Eidolon helvum (confirmed reservoir of henipaviruses) and Epomophorus gambianus (putative reservoir of filoviruses). Using a Luminex-based multiplex microsphere immunoassay and a pseudotype virus neutralization assay, we observed an increase in antibodies against the Nipah glycoprotein in most bats that received the Nipah vaccine. In contrast, we could only detect anti-Ebola glycoprotein antibodies in one bat species, Ep. gambianus, in response to the Ebola vaccine. Transcriptomic analysis in Ei. helvum revealed that genes involved in ISGylation and T-cell differentiation pathways were differentially expressed in response to the Ebola, but not the Nipah, vaccine. These results demonstrate species-specific differences in responses to antigen exposure, offering insights into bat adaptive immune systems and providing calibration for serological data from wild bats.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the knowledge gap regarding antibody production and gene expression in bats following exposure to vaccines encoding Ebola or Nipah virus antigens, as detecting viruses in bats is difficult and the significance of antibodies remains unclear without in vivo immunological studies.
Design and population
The study involved measuring antibody production and gene expression in two African bat species, Eidolon helvum (confirmed reservoir of henipaviruses) and Epomophorus gambianus (putative reservoir of filoviruses), following injection with vaccines expressing either a Nipah virus G glycoprotein or an Ebola virus GP glycoprotein.
Core methods
Antibody production was assessed using a Luminex-based multiplex microsphere immunoassay and a pseudotype virus neutralization assay, while transcriptomic analysis was conducted to evaluate gene expression changes in response to the vaccines.
Main results
Most bats that received the Nipah vaccine showed an increase in antibodies against the Nipah glycoprotein, whereas only one bat species, Epomophorus gambianus, exhibited detectable anti-Ebola glycoprotein antibodies in response to the Ebola vaccine. Transcriptomic analysis in Eidolon helvum revealed differential expression of genes involved in ISGylation and T-cell differentiation pathways following the Ebola vaccine, but not the Nipah vaccine.
Interpretation and novelty
The results demonstrate species-specific differences in immune responses to antigen exposure, providing insights into the adaptive immune systems of bats and offering calibration for serological data from wild bat populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer valuable insights into bat immune responses to filovirus antigens, which can inform the interpretation of serological data from wild bats and contribute to understanding the transmission dynamics of Ebola viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on a small number of bat species and individuals, which may affect generalisability. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed in vivo immunological studies.

K4DD 埃博拉病毒证据和讨论摘要第 11 号

K4DD Ebola Evidence and Discourse Summary No 11
作者： Ismael Kawooya
摘要中文翻译埃博拉健康证据和讨论摘要旨在为 FCDO 和其他机构指明有关正在进行的 埃博拉病毒病 暴发 的最新相关证据和讨论。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
埃博拉健康证据和讨论摘要旨在为 FCDO 和其他机构指明有关正在进行的 埃博拉病毒病 暴发 的最新相关证据和讨论。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
提供的证据没有明确说明直接观察到的主要结果。
解释与创新
所提供的证据没有清楚地区分作者的解释或研究的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义

局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Journal: Emerging infectious diseases  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-01  |  DOI: 10.3201/eid3210.261411  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 32, 10, 1543-1550

跨境本迪布焦病毒病 暴发 期间检测、通知和响应及时性的早期行动审查,乌干达,2026 年。

Early Action Review of Detection, Notification, and Response Timeliness during Cross-Border Bundibugyo Virus Disease Outbreak, Uganda, 2026.
作者： Joshua Kayiwa, Mudarshiru Bbuye, Ritah Atuhaire, Doreen Nyakato, Mathew Tut, Bayad Nozad, Jovan Baryamujura, Bernard Lubwama, Solome Okware, Pamela Kajumba
摘要中文翻译本迪布焦病毒病 (BVD) 是一种没有获得许可的 疫苗 或治疗药物的 埃博拉病毒 物种,于 2026 年 5 月在乌干达和刚果民主共和国作为跨境 暴发 重新出现。在 2026 年 7 月 8 日至 9 日为期 2 天的研讨会期间,我们使用 7-1-7 框架(7 天检测、1 天通知、7 天完成早期响应行动)对 暴发 响应进行了早期行动审查,以评估及时性并确定 9 个响应支柱的瓶颈和推动因素。乌干达于2026年5月15日宣布其暴发;截至7月8日,该国已报告确诊病例20例(其中输入性病例15例,本土传播病例5例),病死率为15%。乌干达实现了全部 3 个目标:6 天内检测、不到 1 天内通知、2 天内完成响应。临床怀疑度低、跨境数据共享差距、分散的数字系统和社区参与延迟是常见的瓶颈;强有力的领导和协调结构是最常被提及的促成因素。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
此次审查的重点是乌干达和刚果民主共和国跨境本迪布焦病毒病 暴发 期间的检测、通知和响应及时性。核心问题是评估 7-1-7 框架内 暴发 响应行动的有效性,并确定 9 个响应支柱的瓶颈和推动因素。
证据基础与方法
此次审查是在 2026 年 7 月 8 日至 9 日为期 2 天的研讨会期间作为早期行动审查进行的,使用 7-1-7 框架来评估及时性并确定瓶颈和推动因素。证据包括有关 暴发 声明、病例数和病死率的数据,以及应对挑战和成功的定性评估。
共识与结论
乌干达实现了 7-1-7 框架的所有三个目标:6 天内发现、1 天内通知、2 天内完成响应。据报道,病死率为15%,确诊病例20例,其中15例为输入性病例,5例为本地传播病例。
争议与缺口
该审查强调了常见的瓶颈,例如临床怀疑度低、跨境数据共享差距、分散的数字系统和社区参与延迟。然而,证据并未提供对这些问题或其对 暴发 结果影响的详细分析。
科研与实践启示
该审查建议需要改善临床怀疑、增强跨境数据共享机制以及更好地集成数字系统,以支持及时的 暴发 响应。它还强调了强有力的领导和协调结构对于有效管理 暴发 的重要性。

Early Action Review of Detection, Notification, and Response Timeliness during Cross-Border Bundibugyo Virus Disease Outbreak, Uganda, 2026.

Authors: Joshua Kayiwa, Mudarshiru Bbuye, Ritah Atuhaire, Doreen Nyakato, Mathew Tut, Bayad Nozad, Jovan Baryamujura, Bernard Lubwama, Solome Okware, Pamela Kajumba
Original abstract (English)
Bundibugyo virus disease (BVD), an Ebola virus species with no licensed vaccine or therapeutic, reemerged in May 2026 as a cross-border outbreak in Uganda and the Democratic Republic of the Congo. During a 2-day workshop, July 8-9, 2026, we conducted an early action review of the outbreak response using the 7-1-7 framework (7 days to detect, 1 day to notify, 7 days to complete early response actions) to assess timeliness and identify bottlenecks and enablers across 9 response pillars. Uganda declared its outbreak on May 15, 2026; by July 8, the country had recorded 20 confirmed cases (15 imported, 5 locally transmitted) and a case-fatality rate of 15%. Uganda met all 3 targets: detection in 6 days, notification in <1 day, and response completion in 2 days. Low clinical suspicion, cross-border data-sharing gaps, fragmented digital systems, and delayed community engagement were common bottlenecks; strong leadership and coordination structures were most cited enablers.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the detection, notification, and response timeliness during a cross-border Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in Uganda and the Democratic Republic of the Congo. The central question is to evaluate the effectiveness of outbreak response actions within the 7-1-7 framework and identify bottlenecks and enablers across 9 response pillars.
Evidence base and method
The review was conducted as an early action review during a 2-day workshop on July 8-9, 2026, using the 7-1-7 framework to assess timeliness and identify bottlenecks and enablers. The evidence includes data on outbreak declaration, case counts, and case-fatality rates, as well as qualitative assessments of response challenges and successes.
Consensus and conclusions
Uganda met all three targets of the 7-1-7 framework: detection within 6 days, notification within less than 1 day, and response completion within 2 days. The case-fatality rate was reported as 15%, with 20 confirmed cases, 15 of which were imported and 5 locally transmitted.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights common bottlenecks such as low clinical suspicion, cross-border data-sharing gaps, fragmented digital systems, and delayed community engagement. However, the evidence does not provide detailed analysis of these issues or their impact on outbreak outcomes.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests the need for improved clinical suspicion, enhanced cross-border data-sharing mechanisms, and better integration of digital systems to support timely outbreak response. It also emphasizes the importance of strong leadership and coordination structures in managing outbreaks effectively.

预测 Ervebo 对 Bundibugyo 病毒病的功效

Predicting the efficacy of Ervebo against Bundibugyo virus disease
作者： Karen M Elias, Ece Egilmezer, Shanchita R. Khan, Ainslie Mitchell, Arnold Reynaldi, Matthew T Berry, Timothy E. Schlub, Bronwyn A. Bailey, Deborah Cromer, Tari Turner
摘要中文翻译目前尚无唯一获得许可的埃博拉病毒病 疫苗(Ervebo)对抗本迪布焦病毒病有效性的临床数据。多项研究表明,Ervebo 疫苗接种 后与 Bundibugyo 病毒发生免疫交叉反应。然而,Bundibugyo 的抗体识别率比 埃博拉病毒 的识别率低约 2.8 倍。在这里,我们的目的是基于对 Ervebo 免疫原性和 埃博拉病毒病 保护的数据分析,推断免疫识别能力下降 2.8 倍可能如何影响 Ervebo 对 Bundibugyo 病毒病的保护。这项工作有三个主要组成部分。首先,我们分析了 Ervebo 关键 环形疫苗接种 临床试验中 疫苗 保护的时机。其次,我们对 Ervebo 疫苗接种 后一段时间内对 埃博拉病毒 的抗体反应进行了系统回顾和荟萃分析。第三,我们将这些数据与先前报告的与 Bundibugyo 病毒交叉反应的数据相结合,推断 Ervebo 对 Bundibugyo 病毒病的保护作用。我们发现 Ervebo 的临床试验数据支持 疫苗 保护早于 疫苗接种 后 10 天开始。其次,疫苗接种后第28天针对埃博拉病毒的结合抗体水平(峰值响应)平均比第7天高12.9倍(95%置信区间,CI:6.4 - 26.0),比第10天高6.3倍(95% CI:3.7 -10.8),比第10天高2.5倍(95% CI:1.4 - 4.4) 高于第 14 天(中和抗体显示相似的结果)。最后,我们考虑了第 7 天或第 10 天的 埃博拉病毒 抗体水平是针对 埃博拉病毒病 的推定保护阈值的情况,并且我们假设这些阈值也适用于本迪布焦病毒病。然后,与 埃博拉病毒 相比,对 Bundibugyo 的反应减少了 2.8 倍,我们预测 Ervebo 疫苗接种 后对 Bundibugyo 病毒的抗体反应可能会高于这些假定的保护阈值。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是预测 Ervebo 对 Bundibugyo 病毒病的功效。核心问题是,与 埃博拉病毒 相比,本迪布焦病毒的免疫识别能力下降 2.8 倍,这可能会如何影响 Ervebo 对本迪布焦病毒病的保护能力。主题范围仅限于人类致病性正埃博拉病毒,以本迪布焦病毒和 埃博拉病毒 为主要目标。
证据基础与方法
该综述包括对来自关键 环形疫苗接种 临床试验的 Ervebo 免疫原性数据的系统分析、对 埃博拉病毒 随时间推移的抗体反应的荟萃分析,以及与之前报告的与 Bundibugyo 病毒的交叉反应数据的比较。这些方法涉及检查 疫苗 保护的时间并量化 疫苗接种 后不同时间点的抗体水平。
共识与结论
与 埃博拉病毒 相比,Ervebo 对 Bundibugyo 病毒病的保护作用可能受到 Bundibugyo 抗体识别率低 2.8 倍的影响。这表明,Ervebo 疫苗接种 后对本迪布焦病毒的抗体反应可能高于基于 埃博拉病毒病 数据的推定保护阈值。
争议与缺口
该审查承认,Ervebo 对 Bundibugyo 病毒病功效的证据仅限于基于 埃博拉病毒 的交叉反应性和免疫原性研究的推断数据。目前还没有关于 Ervebo 对 Bundibugyo 病毒病有效性的直接临床数据,这造成了证据空白。
科研与实践启示
该评论强调需要进一步研究以直接评估 Ervebo 对 Bundibugyo 病毒病的功效。它还建议未来的研究应侧重于了解 Bundibugyo 病毒的保护阈值,并验证这些阈值与 埃博拉病毒病 的阈值相似的假设。

Predicting the efficacy of Ervebo against Bundibugyo virus disease

Authors: Karen M Elias, Ece Egilmezer, Shanchita R. Khan, Ainslie Mitchell, Arnold Reynaldi, Matthew T Berry, Timothy E. Schlub, Bronwyn A. Bailey, Deborah Cromer, Tari Turner
Original abstract (English)
There is currently no clinical data for the effectiveness of the only licensed Ebola virus disease vaccine (Ervebo) against Bundibugyo virus disease. A number of studies have shown immune cross-reactivity with Bundibugyo virus after Ervebo vaccination. However, antibody recognition of Bundibugyo is around 2.8-fold lower than recognition of Ebola virus. Here, we aimed to infer how a 2.8-fold drop in immune recognition may affect Ervebo protection against Bundibugyo virus disease based on analysis of data on Ervebo immunogenicity and protection from Ebola virus disease. This work has three main components. Firstly, we analysed the timing of vaccine protection in the Ervebo pivotal ring vaccination clinical trial. Secondly, we performed a systematic review and meta-analysis of antibody responses to Ebola virus after Ervebo vaccination over time. Thirdly, we combined these data with previously reported data on the cross-reactivity to Bundibugyo virus to infer protection of Ervebo against Bundibugyo virus disease. We find that the clinical trial data for Ervebo supports vaccine protection beginning earlier than 10 days after vaccination. Secondly, binding antibody levels against Ebola virus on day 28 post vaccination (peak responses) were on average 12.9-fold (95% confidence interval, CI: 6.4 - 26.0) higher than on day 7, 6.3-fold (95% CI: 3.7 -10.8) higher than on day 10, and 2.5-fold (95% CI: 1.4 - 4.4) higher than on day 14 (neutralising antibodies showed similar results). Finally, we consider the scenarios where antibody levels to Ebola virus at day 7 or 10 are putative protective thresholds against Ebola virus disease, and we assume these thresholds also apply for Bundibugyo virus disease. Then, with a 2.8-fold reduction of responses to Bundibugyo compared to Ebola virus, we predict antibody responses to Bundibugyo virus after Ervebo vaccination are likely above these putative protective thresholds.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on predicting the efficacy of Ervebo against Bundibugyo virus disease. The central question is how a 2.8-fold drop in immune recognition of Bundibugyo virus compared to Ebola virus may affect Ervebo's protective capacity against Bundibugyo virus disease. The topic boundary is limited to human-pathogenic orthoebolaviruses, with Bundibugyo virus and Ebola virus as the primary targets.
Evidence base and method
The review includes a systematic analysis of Ervebo's immunogenicity data from the pivotal ring vaccination clinical trial, a meta-analysis of antibody responses to Ebola virus over time, and a comparison with previously reported cross-reactivity data to Bundibugyo virus. The methods involve examining the timing of vaccine protection and quantifying antibody levels at different time points post-vaccination.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that Ervebo's protection against Bundibugyo virus disease may be influenced by the 2.8-fold lower antibody recognition of Bundibugyo compared to Ebola virus. It suggests that antibody responses to Bundibugyo virus after Ervebo vaccination are likely above putative protective thresholds based on data from Ebola virus disease.
Controversies and gaps
The review acknowledges that the evidence for Ervebo's efficacy against Bundibugyo virus disease is limited to inferred data based on cross-reactivity and immunogenicity studies of Ebola virus. There is no direct clinical data on the effectiveness of Ervebo against Bundibugyo virus disease, which creates an evidence gap.
Research and practice implications
The review highlights the need for further research to directly assess Ervebo's efficacy against Bundibugyo virus disease. It also suggests that future studies should focus on understanding the protective thresholds for Bundibugyo virus and validating the assumption that these thresholds are similar to those for Ebola virus disease.

Journal: Journal of Integrative Medicine and Research  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-01  |  DOI: 10.4103/jimr.jimr_111_26  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 4, 4, 270-279
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

埃博拉病毒病 通过单一健康视角:整合人类、动物和环境健康以做好准备、预防和控制 – 叙述性回顾

Ebola virus disease through the one health lens: Integrating human, animal and environmental health for preparedness, prevention, and control – A narrative review
作者： S Suneeti Kanyari, Sangram Panda, Geeta Chand Acharya, Jyoti Ranjan Sahoo, Abhipsa Mohanty
摘要中文翻译埃博拉病毒病 (EVD) 是一种严重且往往致命的人畜共患疾病,继续对公共卫生构成严重挑战,特别是在中非和西非。尽管在诊断、感染预防、疫苗接种和治疗方面取得了重大进展,但埃博拉病毒暴发一再暴露出监测、准备、生态风险监测和社区信任方面的差距。这种疾病不仅仅由病毒引起;它的出现和传播是由野生动物、环境变化、社会文化实践、人口流动和脆弱的卫生系统之间的相互作用决定的。通过 One Health 视角观察 EVD 有助于更清楚地理解这些联系。人类健康、动物健康和生态系统健康密切相关,埃博拉病毒的预防不能仅依靠病例出现后的临床护理。有效的控制需要及早发现人类疾病,监测野生动物和生态风险,强大的实验室系统,卫生设施中的感染预防,及时的治疗,安全和有尊严的埋葬做法,疫苗接种(如果适用)和有意义的社区参与。风险沟通必须基于信任、尊重和当地参与,特别是在护理和丧葬习俗深深植根于社会和文化生活的社区。尽管最近的进展改善了埃博拉控制的前景,但仍然存在重大挑战。部门间协调薄弱、资金不足、兽医公共卫生能力有限、不安全、数据系统分散和社区信心不足继续影响备灾和应对工作。因此,在地方、国家和全球各级加强同一个健康平台至关重要,不仅对于埃博拉病毒病,而且对于快速变化的全球格局中未来新出现和重新出现的人畜共患疾病也至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
埃博拉病毒病 (EVD) 是一种严重且往往致命的人畜共患疾病,继续对公共卫生构成严重挑战,特别是在中非和西非。这种疾病不仅仅由病毒引起;它的出现和传播是由野生动物、环境变化、社会文化实践、人口流动和脆弱的卫生系统之间的相互作用决定的。
证据基础与方法
该评论是一篇综合了人类、动物和环境健康观点的叙述性评论。它不报告特定的数据库、搜索日期或资格标准。该评论包括对该主题的一般性讨论,没有荟萃分析或共识过程细节。
共识与结论
通过 One Health 视角观察 EVD 有助于更清楚地理解这些联系。人类健康、动物健康和生态系统健康密切相关,埃博拉病毒的预防不能仅依靠病例出现后的临床护理。
争议与缺口
部门间协调薄弱、资金不足、兽医公共卫生能力有限、不安全、数据系统分散和社区信心不足继续影响备灾和应对工作。审查指出,最近的进展改善了埃博拉控制的前景,但仍然存在重要挑战。
科研与实践启示
有效的控制需要及早发现人类疾病,监测野生动物和生态风险,强大的实验室系统,卫生设施中的感染预防,及时的治疗,安全和有尊严的埋葬做法,疫苗接种(如果适用)和有意义的社区参与。风险沟通必须基于信任、尊重和当地参与,特别是在护理和丧葬习俗深深植根于社会和文化生活的社区。因此,在地方、国家和全球各级加强同一个健康平台至关重要,不仅对于埃博拉病毒病,而且对于快速变化的全球格局中未来新出现和重新出现的人畜共患疾病也至关重要。

Ebola virus disease through the one health lens: Integrating human, animal and environmental health for preparedness, prevention, and control – A narrative review

Authors: S Suneeti Kanyari, Sangram Panda, Geeta Chand Acharya, Jyoti Ranjan Sahoo, Abhipsa Mohanty
Original abstract (English)
Ebola virus disease (EVD) is a severe, often fatal zoonotic disease that continues to pose a serious public health challenge, especially in Central and West Africa. Despite major progress in diagnostics, infection prevention, vaccination, and therapeutics, Ebola outbreaks repeatedly reveal gaps in surveillance, preparedness, ecological risk monitoring, and community trust. The disease is not driven by the virus alone; its emergence and spread are shaped by interactions between wildlife reservoirs, environmental change, sociocultural practices, population movement, and fragile health systems. Viewing EVD through a One Health lens helps in understanding these connections more clearly. Human health, animal health and ecosystem health are closely linked, and Ebola prevention cannot depend only on clinical care after cases appear. Effective control requires early detection of human illness, monitoring of wildlife and ecological risks, strong laboratory systems, infection prevention in health facilities, timely treatment, safe and dignified burial practices, vaccination where applicable and meaningful community engagement. Risk communication must be based on trust, respect and local participation, particularly in communities where caregiving and funeral practices are deeply rooted in social and cultural life. Although recent advances have improved the outlook for Ebola control, important challenges remain. Weak intersectoral coordination, inadequate financing, limited veterinary public health capacity, insecurity, fragmented data systems and low community confidence continue to affect preparedness and response. Strengthening One Health platforms at local, national and global levels is therefore essential, not only for EVD, but also for future emerging and re-emerging zoonotic diseases in a rapidly changing global landscape.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Ebola virus disease (EVD) is a severe, often fatal zoonotic disease that continues to pose a serious public health challenge, especially in Central and West Africa. The disease is not driven by the virus alone; its emergence and spread are shaped by interactions between wildlife reservoirs, environmental change, sociocultural practices, population movement, and fragile health systems.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative review that integrates human, animal, and environmental health perspectives. It does not report specific databases, search dates, or eligibility criteria. The review includes a general discussion of the topic without meta-analysis or consensus process details.
Consensus and conclusions
Viewing EVD through a One Health lens helps in understanding these connections more clearly. Human health, animal health and ecosystem health are closely linked, and Ebola prevention cannot depend only on clinical care after cases appear.
Controversies and gaps
Weak intersectoral coordination, inadequate financing, limited veterinary public health capacity, insecurity, fragmented data systems and low community confidence continue to affect preparedness and response. The review notes that recent advances have improved the outlook for Ebola control, but important challenges remain.
Research and practice implications
Effective control requires early detection of human illness, monitoring of wildlife and ecological risks, strong laboratory systems, infection prevention in health facilities, timely treatment, safe and dignified burial practices, vaccination where applicable and meaningful community engagement. Risk communication must be based on trust, respect and local participation, particularly in communities where caregiving and funeral practices are deeply rooted in social and cultural life. Strengthening One Health platforms at local, national and global levels is therefore essential, not only for EVD, but also for future emerging and re-emerging zoonotic diseases in a rapidly changing global landscape.

埃博拉病:2026 年本迪布焦病毒 暴发 背景下的最新综述

Ebola Disease: An Updated Review in the Context of the 2026 Bundibugyo Virus Outbreak
作者： Fahriye Melis Gürsoy Kurtoğlu, Aliye Baştuğ, Fahriye Melis Gürsoy
摘要中文翻译本综述全面概述了当前的术语和分类、发病机制、传播 模式、临床和实验室检查结果、诊断方法、病例定义、治疗 方法、感染预防和控制 (IPC) 措施、接触者追踪、疫苗接种 策略以及与 EBOD 相关的长期后遗症。此外,由于分析仅包括埃博拉病毒 暴发,因此应谨慎地将这些发现外推至由其他正埃博拉病毒物种引起的 暴发。总而言之,这些发现支持“同一个健康”方法,将人类疾病 监测 与动物和环境监测相结合,同时促进公共卫生、兽医、野生动物和环境当局之间的协调。摘要 埃博拉病(EBOD)是一种严重的病毒性疾病,由丝状病毒科正埃博拉病毒属病毒引起,发病率很高。2026年5月15日,刚果民主共和国伊图里省出现由本迪布焦病毒(BDBV)引起的新暴发,加上乌干达发现跨境病例,再次凸显了EBOD对全球公共卫生的重要性。目前获得许可的 疫苗 和单克隆抗体疗法仅批准用于扎伊尔正博拉病毒,限制了 BDBV 暴发 期间的 治疗 选择。加速泛丝状病毒 疫苗 和疗法的开发、加强早期诊断能力和改善 IPC 基础设施仍然是关键优先事项。简介 定义、分类和当前术语 埃博拉病 (EBOD) 是一种由丝状病毒科正埃博拉病毒属病毒引起的严重病毒性疾病。根据目前的分类,主要的正埃博拉病毒种类...
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
埃博拉病 (EBOD) 是一种严重的病毒性疾病,由丝状病毒科正埃博拉病毒属病毒引起,发病率很高。该综述重点关注当前的术语和分类、发病机制、传播 模式、临床和实验室检查结果、诊断方法、病例定义、治疗 方法、感染预防和控制 (IPC) 措施、接触者追踪、疫苗接种 策略以及与 EBOD 相关的长期后遗症。核心问题是在 2026 本迪布焦病毒 暴发 的背景下评估 EBOD 的流行病学、临床和治疗方面。
证据基础与方法
该综述基于对文献的综合分析,包括刚果民主共和国的2026年本迪布焦病毒暴发和乌干达的跨境病例。证据包括 暴发 的历史数据、临床发现和公共卫生影响。该评论没有明确报告荟萃分析或共识过程,但它提供了当前对 EBOD 理解的详细概述。
共识与结论
2014-2016 年西非 暴发 最初在几内亚报告,随后传播到利比里亚和塞拉利昂,仍然是迄今为止记录的最大埃博拉病毒病 暴发,病例数超过 28,000 例,死亡人数 11,308 人。由不同正埃博拉病毒属引起的疾病分别被指定为埃博拉病毒病 (EVD)、苏丹病毒病(SVD)、本迪布焦病毒病(BVD)和塔伊森林病毒病。“埃博拉病”一词是对由不同正埃博拉病毒属引起的疾病的广义称呼。
争议与缺口
该审查强调,目前获得许可的 疫苗 和单克隆抗体疗法仅批准用于扎伊尔正博拉病毒,限制了 BDBV 暴发 期间 治疗 的选择。死亡率 根据致病病毒种类、获得医疗保健的机会以及个体 暴发 的流行病学和临床特征而有所不同。审查还指出,在非洲、欧洲和北美发现的与旅行相关的病例进一步凸显了该疾病国际传播的可能性。
科研与实践启示
加快泛丝状病毒 疫苗 和治疗方法的开发、加强早期诊断能力以及改善感染预防和控制 (IPC) 基础设施仍然是关键优先事项。2026年5月15日,刚果民主共和国伊图里省出现由本迪布焦病毒(BDBV)引起的新暴发,加上乌干达发现跨境病例,再次凸显了EBOD对全球公共卫生的重要性。有限的医疗保健能力、不足的监测系统、感染预防和控制(IPC)的缺陷、城市传播以及广泛的人口流动导致了2014-2016年西非暴发的快速扩张。

Ebola Disease: An Updated Review in the Context of the 2026 Bundibugyo Virus Outbreak

Authors: Fahriye Melis Gürsoy Kurtoğlu, Aliye Baştuğ, Fahriye Melis Gürsoy
Original abstract (English)
This review provides a comprehensive overview of the current terminology and classification, pathogenesis, modes of transmission, clinical and laboratory findings, diagnostic methods, case definitions, treatment approaches, Infection prevention and control (IPC) measures, contact tracing, vaccination strategies, and long-term sequelae associated with EBOD. Moreover, because the analysis included only EBOV outbreaks, these findings should be extrapolated cautiously to outbreaks caused by other Orthoebolavirus species. Taken together, these findings support a One Health approach that integrates human disease surveillance with animal and environmental monitoring while fostering coordination among public health, veterinary, wildlife, and environmental authorities. Abstract Ebola disease (EBOD) is a severe viral illness with a high mortality rate caused by viruses of the genus Orthoebolavirus within the family Filoviridae. The emergence of a new outbreak caused by Bundibugyo virus (BDBV) in Ituri Province of the Democratic Republic of the Congo on May 15, 2026, along with the detection of cross-border cases in Uganda, has once again underscored the global public health significance of EBOD. Currently licensed vaccines and monoclonal antibody therapies are approved only for Orthoebolavirus zairense, limiting treatment options during BDBV outbreaks. Accelerating the development of pan-filovirus vaccines and therapeutics, strengthening early diagnostic capacity, and improving IPC infrastructure remain key priorities. Introduction Definition, Classification, and Current Terminology Ebola disease (EBOD) is a severe viral illness caused by viruses of the genus Orthoebolavirus within the family Filoviridae. Under the current classification, the principal Orthoebolavirus species kn...
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Ebola disease (EBOD) is a severe viral illness with a high mortality rate caused by viruses of the genus Orthoebolavirus within the family Filoviridae. The review focuses on the current terminology and classification, pathogenesis, modes of transmission, clinical and laboratory findings, diagnostic methods, case definitions, treatment approaches, infection prevention and control (IPC) measures, contact tracing, vaccination strategies, and long-term sequelae associated with EBOD. The central question is to evaluate the epidemiological, clinical, and therapeutic aspects of EBOD in the context of the 2026 Bundibugyo virus outbreak.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on a comprehensive analysis of the literature, including the 2026 Bundibugyo virus outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and cross-border cases in Uganda. The evidence includes historical data on outbreaks, clinical findings, and public health implications. The review does not explicitly report on meta-analysis or consensus processes, but it provides a detailed overview of the current understanding of EBOD.
Consensus and conclusions
The 2014-2016 West African outbreak, initially reported in Guinea and subsequently spreading to Liberia and Sierra Leone, remains the largest EVD outbreak recorded to date, with more than 28,000 cases and 11,308 deaths. The diseases caused by different Orthoebolavirus species are designated as Ebola virus disease (EVD), Sudan virus disease (SVD), Bundibugyo virus disease (BVD), and Taï Forest virus disease, respectively. The term 'Ebola disease' is used as a broad designation for diseases caused by different Orthoebolavirus species.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that currently licensed vaccines and monoclonal antibody therapies are approved only for Orthoebolavirus zairense, limiting treatment options during BDBV outbreaks. The mortality varies according to the causative viral species, access to healthcare, and the epidemiological and clinical characteristics of individual outbreaks. The review also notes that travel-associated cases identified in Africa, Europe, and North America further underscored the potential for international dissemination of the disease.
Research and practice implications
Accelerating the development of pan-filovirus vaccines and therapeutics, strengthening early diagnostic capacity, and improving infection prevention and control (IPC) infrastructure remain key priorities. The emergence of a new outbreak caused by Bundibugyo virus (BDBV) in Ituri Province of the Democratic Republic of the Congo on May 15, 2026, along with the detection of cross-border cases in Uganda, has once again underscored the global public health significance of EBOD. Limited healthcare capacity, inadequate surveillance systems, deficiencies in infection prevention and control (IPC), urban transmission, and extensive population mobility contributed to the rapid expansion of the 2014-2016 West African outbreak.

内质网吞噬的细胞内病原体操纵:直接受体靶向和间接途径扰动。

Intracellular pathogen manipulation of ER-phagy: direct receptor targeting and indirect pathway perturbation.
作者： Xiaoqi Lu, Yang Yang, Ranhui Li, Yating Wen
摘要中文翻译内质网选择性自噬(ER-phagy)通过选择性去除受损的内质网膜和错误折叠的蛋白质来维持细胞内稳态。这篇综述描述了经典自噬的核心分子机制,然后系统地描述了三种类型的内质网自噬(大内质网自噬、微内质网自噬和囊泡内质网自噬)、它们的特征受体、子域特异性和调节机制,同时对酵母和哺乳动物内质网自噬受体系统之间的进化差异进行了比较分析。在直接调控水平上,研究了针对 ER 吞噬受体的多种细胞内病原体策略,包括病毒对 FAM134B 的蛋白酶体降解和空间隔离、逆转录病毒的非典型微 ER 吞噬劫持、FAM134B 针对 埃博拉病毒 介导的组成型 抗病毒药物 防御,以及细菌效应子和病原体相关分子模式对该受体的双向调节。在间接调控水平上,总结了细胞内病原体介导的上游节点扰动,包括 UPR、ERAD、ER-RQC/UFMylation 和核心巨自噬机制。这些发现强调了 ER-自噬作为 宿主 防御屏障和病原体利用目标的双重作用,为细胞内致病机制提供了新的见解。

Intracellular pathogen manipulation of ER-phagy: direct receptor targeting and indirect pathway perturbation.

Authors: Xiaoqi Lu, Yang Yang, Ranhui Li, Yating Wen
Original abstract (English)
Selective autophagy of the endoplasmic reticulum (ER-phagy) maintains intracellular homeostasis by selectively removing damaged ER membranes and contained misfolded proteins. This review delineates the core molecular mechanisms of canonical autophagy, then systematically describes three types of ER-phagy (macro-ER-phagy, micro-ER-phagy, and vesicular ER-phagy), their signature receptors, subdomain specificities, and regulatory mechanisms, alongside a comparative analysis of the evolutionary divergence between yeast and mammalian ER-phagy receptor systems. At the direct regulation level, diverse intracellular pathogen strategies targeting ER-phagy receptors are examined, including proteasomal degradation and spatial sequestration of FAM134B by viruses, non-canonical micro-ER-phagy hijacking by retroviruses, constitutive antiviral defense mediated by FAM134B against Ebola virus, and bidirectional modulation of this receptor by bacterial effectors and pathogen-associated molecular patterns. At the indirect regulation level, intracellular pathogen-mediated perturbation of upstream nodes-including the UPR, ERAD, ER-RQC/UFMylation, and core macroautophagy machinery-is summarized. These findings underscore the dual role of ER-phagy as both a host defense barrier and a target for pathogen exploitation, providing novel insights into intracellular pathogenic mechanism.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review delineates the core molecular mechanisms of canonical autophagy, then systematically describes three types of ER-phagy (macro-ER-phagy, micro-ER-phagy, and vesicular ER-phagy), their signature receptors, subdomain specificities, and regulatory mechanisms, alongside a comparative analysis of the evolutionary divergence between yeast and mammalian ER-phagy receptor systems.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions
At the direct regulation level, diverse intracellular pathogen strategies targeting ER-phagy receptors are examined, including proteasomal degradation and spatial sequestration of FAM134B by viruses, non-canonical micro-ER-phagy hijacking by retroviruses, constitutive antiviral defense mediated by FAM134B against Ebola virus, and bidirectional modulation of this receptor by bacterial effectors and pathogen-associated molecular patterns.
Controversies and gaps

Research and practice implications
At the indirect regulation level, intracellular pathogen-mediated perturbation of upstream nodes-including the UPR, ERAD, ER-RQC/UFMylation, and core macroautophagy machinery-is summarized.

疫苗接种 悖论:东非在埃博拉 疫苗 准备工作中取得的成功如何可能为未来创造新的公众期望 暴发

The vaccination paradox: how East Africa’s success in Ebola vaccine preparedness may be creating new public expectations for future outbreaks
作者： Okechukwu Paul-Chima Ugwu, Chinyere Nneoma Ugwu, Godson Emeka Anyanwu, Mariam Basajja, Maryann Chiamaka Ebuoh, Ugwu Okechukwu Paul-Chima
摘要中文翻译这一观点综合了已发表的有关非洲埃博拉病毒 疫苗接种 和 暴发 防范的证据。疫苗接种 成功对抗扎伊尔埃博拉病毒加强了基于 疫苗 的 暴发 控制,但当公众期望超过特定物种 疫苗 的可用性时,也可能会产生准备风险。我们提出 疫苗 期望差距、疫苗 预期和特定物种 疫苗 素养作为相互关联的假设生成结构,并由准备信托合同支持以进行实际实施。该框架借鉴了预期否定以及危机和紧急情况风险沟通理论,并将既定证据与解释和可检验的假设区分开来。最近的苏丹病毒和本迪布焦病毒 暴发 说明了这一差距的相关性,因为获得许可的 疫苗 仍然具有物种特异性。证据集中在乌干达,这使得更广泛的东非应用成为需要实证检验的假设。如果得到验证,该框架可以加强风险沟通、社区信任和区域卫生安全规划。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查探讨了东非在埃博拉病毒 疫苗 防范方面取得的成功,特别是针对扎伊尔埃博拉病毒,可能会如何影响公众对未来 暴发 的期望。它重点关注 疫苗 可用性和管理 埃博拉病毒病 及相关 暴发 的准备情况的影响。
证据基础与方法
该审查综合了已发表的有关非洲埃博拉病毒 疫苗接种 和 暴发 防范的证据。它借鉴了期望否定和危机沟通理论,区分既定证据和解释。
共识与结论
该审查强调了当公众期望超过特定物种 疫苗 的可用性时,可能会出现“疫苗 期望差距”。它提出了诸如“疫苗 预期”和“特定物种 疫苗 素养”之类的结构,作为改善准备和信任的工具。
争议与缺口
证据集中在乌干达,限制了研究结果对更广泛的东非的推广。该框架被提出为假设生成,需要实证验证以加强其在公共卫生和风险沟通中的应用。
科研与实践启示
审查表明,如果该框架得到验证,可以增强风险沟通、社区信任和区域卫生安全规划。它强调需要对所提出的结构进行实证测试和实际实施。

The vaccination paradox: how East Africa’s success in Ebola vaccine preparedness may be creating new public expectations for future outbreaks

Authors: Okechukwu Paul-Chima Ugwu, Chinyere Nneoma Ugwu, Godson Emeka Anyanwu, Mariam Basajja, Maryann Chiamaka Ebuoh, Ugwu Okechukwu Paul-Chima
Original abstract (English)
This perspective synthesizes published evidence on Ebola vaccination and outbreak preparedness in Africa. Successful vaccination against Zaire ebolavirus has strengthened vaccine-based outbreak control but may also create a preparedness risk when public expectations exceed species-specific vaccine availability. We propose the Vaccine Expectation Gap, Vaccine Anticipation and Species-Specific Vaccine Literacy as linked, hypothesis-generating constructs, supported by a Preparedness Trust Contract for practical implementation. The framework draws on expectation-disconfirmation and crisis and emergency risk communication theories and distinguishes established evidence from interpretation and testable hypotheses. Recent Sudan virus and Bundibugyo virus outbreaks illustrate the relevance of this gap because licensed vaccines remain species-specific. Evidence is concentrated in Uganda, making broader East African application a hypothesis requiring empirical testing. If validated, the framework could strengthen risk communication, community trust and regional health-security planning.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review explores how East Africa's success in Ebola vaccine preparedness, particularly against Zaire ebolavirus, may influence public expectations during future outbreaks. It focuses on the implications of vaccine availability and preparedness for managing Ebola virus disease and related outbreaks.
Evidence base and method
The review synthesizes published evidence on Ebola vaccination and outbreak preparedness in Africa. It draws on theories of expectation-disconfirmation and crisis communication, distinguishing between established evidence and interpretations.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights the potential for a 'Vaccine Expectation Gap' when public expectations exceed the availability of species-specific vaccines. It proposes constructs like 'Vaccine Anticipation' and 'Species-Specific Vaccine Literacy' as tools for improving preparedness and trust.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence is concentrated in Uganda, limiting the generalizability of findings to broader East Africa. The framework is presented as hypothesis-generating, requiring empirical validation to strengthen its application in public health and risk communication.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that the framework could enhance risk communication, community trust, and regional health-security planning if validated. It emphasizes the need for empirical testing and practical implementation of the constructs proposed.

等待的地理:监测 延迟是东非埃博拉风险的隐藏驱动因素

The geography of waiting: surveillance latency as the hidden driver of Ebola risk in East Africa
作者： Okechukwu Paul-Chima Ugwu, Chinyere Nneoma Ugwu, Godson Emeka Anyanwu, Mariam Basajja, Maryann Chiamaka Ebuoh
摘要中文翻译埃博拉病毒病 仍然是东非持续存在的公共卫生威胁,但准备策略继续强调在 暴发 被识别后而不是检测前的时期做出反应。本观点认为,监测 延迟(最早有临床意义的信号与有组织的公共卫生行动之间的累积延迟)是埃博拉风险的一个关键但未充分衡量的决定因素。尽管诊断和报告延迟已被广泛记录,但它们通常被视为孤立的运营挑战。我们引入了等待地理学的概念,这是一个综合框架,将生物、临床、诊断、行政和社区延误与物理、技术和关系基础设施的空间不平等联系起来。诊断、交通、实验室网络、可信通信渠道和社区报告系统的不平等访问导致不同环境下检测时间的巨大差异。如果 监测 延迟延长,传播 可能会继续未被检测到,从而增加了更大、更复杂的 暴发 的可能性。根据乌干达、刚果民主共和国、几内亚和卢旺达 暴发 的证据,我们研究了延迟检测的机制,并将其与乌干达 7-1-7 战略等绩效框架一起考虑。我们提出了一种实用的方法,将症状和基于事件的 监测、分散分子测试、社区预警系统和区域数据共享相结合。由于在此审查的案例中,较短的检测间隔与较小的最终 暴发 相关,因此减少 监测 延迟对于埃博拉病毒防范来说是一项看似合理的高价值投资,尽管这仍然是一个有待正式经济评估的假设,而不是既定的发现。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 埃博拉病毒 及其相关疾病,研究 监测 潜伏期如何影响东非的 暴发 风险。核心问题是解决 监测 延迟是否可以改善准备工作并减少 暴发 的大小。
证据基础与方法
该评论是一篇观点文章,不进行系统或荟萃分析评论。它借鉴了乌干达、刚果民主共和国、几内亚和卢旺达的 暴发 的证据,并考虑了乌干达 7-1-7 战略等绩效框架。该审查不报告研究数量、数据库或正式的资格标准。
共识与结论
监测 潜伏期(定义为最早有临床意义的信号与公共卫生行动之间的累积延迟)是埃博拉风险的一个关键但未充分测量的决定因素。它强调,较短的检测间隔与较小的最终 暴发 相关,这表明减少延迟可能是一项高价值的准备投资。
争议与缺口
审查指出,诊断和报告延迟通常被视为孤立的操作挑战,而不是更广泛的 监测 延迟系统的一部分。它还确定了减少 监测 延迟作为准备策略的正式经济评估中的差距。
科研与实践启示
该评论建议整合基于症状和事件的 监测、分散分子测试、社区预警系统和区域数据共享,以减少 监测 延迟。它表明这些措施可以增强 暴发 的检测和控制,尽管这仍然是一个需要进一步验证的假设。

The geography of waiting: surveillance latency as the hidden driver of Ebola risk in East Africa

Authors: Okechukwu Paul-Chima Ugwu, Chinyere Nneoma Ugwu, Godson Emeka Anyanwu, Mariam Basajja, Maryann Chiamaka Ebuoh
Original abstract (English)
Ebola virus disease remains a persistent public health threat in East Africa, yet preparedness strategies continue to emphasise response after outbreaks are recognised rather than the period preceding detection. This Perspective argues that surveillance latency the cumulative delay between the earliest clinically meaningful signal and organised public health action is a critical but insufficiently measured determinant of Ebola risk. Although diagnostic and reporting delays have been widely documented, they are often treated as isolated operational challenges. We introduce the concept of the geography of waiting, an integrative framework that links biological, clinical, diagnostic, administrative and community delays to spatial inequalities in physical, technical and relational infrastructure. Unequal access to diagnostics, transportation, laboratory networks, trusted communication channels and community reporting systems creates substantial variation in detection timelines across settings. Where surveillance latency is prolonged, transmission may continue undetected, increasing the likelihood of larger and more complex outbreaks. Drawing on evidence from outbreaks in Uganda, the Democratic Republic of the Congo, Guinea and Rwanda, we examine the mechanisms underlying delayed detection and consider them alongside performance frameworks such as Uganda’s 7-1-7 strategy. We propose a practical approach that integrates syndromic and event-based surveillance, decentralised molecular testing, community early-warning systems and regional data sharing. Because shorter detection intervals are associated with smaller eventual outbreaks in the cases reviewed here, reducing surveillance latency is a plausible high-value investment for Ebola preparedness, although this remains a hypothesis pending formal economic evaluation rather than an established finding.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on Ebola viruses and their associated disease, examining how surveillance latency contributes to the risk of outbreaks in East Africa. The central question is whether addressing surveillance latency can improve preparedness and reduce outbreak size.
Evidence base and method
The review is a Perspective article that does not conduct a systematic or meta-analytic review. It draws on evidence from outbreaks in Uganda, the Democratic Republic of the Congo, Guinea, and Rwanda, and considers performance frameworks like Uganda’s 7-1-7 strategy. The review does not report on study counts, databases, or formal eligibility criteria.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that surveillance latency, defined as the cumulative delay between the earliest clinically meaningful signal and public health action, is a critical but insufficiently measured determinant of Ebola risk. It highlights that shorter detection intervals are associated with smaller eventual outbreaks, suggesting that reducing latency could be a high-value investment for preparedness.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that diagnostic and reporting delays are often treated as isolated operational challenges rather than part of a broader system of surveillance latency. It also identifies a gap in formal economic evaluation of reducing surveillance latency as a preparedness strategy.
Research and practice implications
The review proposes integrating syndromic and event-based surveillance, decentralised molecular testing, community early-warning systems, and regional data sharing to reduce surveillance latency. It suggests that these measures could enhance outbreak detection and control, although this remains a hypothesis requiring further validation.
